# Agents · I · part 4

978 entries, implementation-plan.agent to implementer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **implementation-plan.agent**: Collaborative implementation agent. Parses any card format (Jira, GitHub Projects, free-form), generates phased execution plans with tests, and implements step-by-step with human validation gates. Discovers project config, docs, and conventions — never invents paths. Adapts to card structure and locale automatically. ([MatheusFelipeCorrea/Hyperion](https://github.com/MatheusFelipeCorrea/Hyperion/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implementation-plan.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **implementation-plan.agent**: AI agent for collaborative implementation of features and refactoring tasks. Parses any card format (Jira, GitHub Projects, or free-form), generates phased execution plans with unit tests, and implements step-by-step with human validation gates between phases. Uses project documentation (Architecture Blueprint, Exemplars, Folder Structure, Copilot Instructions) as context for consistent, pattern-compliant implementation. Adapts to card structure automatically — supports Portuguese and English, enterprise and indie formats. - **implementer**: Implements exactly one task from a groomed plan (`.plans/{name}/tasks/{nnn}-task-name.md`) following this project's TDD/SOLID/idiomatic-code contract. Resolves its own AGENTS.md and instruction-file scope from the paths it's given — the caller passes paths, not pasted file contents. Never commits, never marks the task done, never touches a second task. Use for the implementation step of /implement-task and /implement-tasks, in both inline and dispatched-subagent mode — inline mode reads this file and follows it directly in the main session; subagent mode dispatches it in isolation. ([aaronlord/.dotfiles](https://github.com/aaronlord/.dotfiles/blob/HEAD/pi/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements a planned ticket until its acceptance tests and the fast checks pass. Use after the test-writer has added failing tests, and again to fix what the verifier or reviewer found. ([abdelhaleemosama/claude-sdlc-plugin](https://github.com/abdelhaleemosama/claude-sdlc-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **Implementer**: Owns repository code changes for execution-ready work and delivers the smallest coherent implementation with local validation. ([ABIvan-Tech/copilot-agentic-workflows-cli](https://github.com/ABIvan-Tech/copilot-agentic-workflows-cli/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implementer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Applies a precise, already-decided change spec — mechanical edits, porting a pattern across files, adding tests, regenerating derived files. Not for root-causing or design decisions. ([adamaslan/nuwrrrld-portal](https://github.com/adamaslan/nuwrrrld-portal/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Writes a well specified change in an area whose owner and pattern are already known: following a skill (add-feature, add-api-endpoint, add-ui-component, add-web-page, add-screener, add-strategy), a TOML expression feature, tests for existing code, a bug fix with a known cause, a doc update. Give it the owner, the files and the acceptance check. Escalates instead of guessing when the change turns out to be a design decision. ([adarbari/algo-trading](https://github.com/adarbari/algo-trading/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Scoped implementation with proportional verification. ([adnanalmahmut/ai-agent](https://github.com/adnanalmahmut/ai-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements one planned task from docs/plan (endpoint, handler, consumer, screen) inside one service. Use for routine feature slices; escalate state machines, money, tenancy and scoring to the main session at high effort. ([afadel151/MiniCrm](https://github.com/afadel151/MiniCrm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
